2097 — separation finger stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2097 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Spreads the fingers wide apart to open up the hand and loosen the small muscles between the fingers.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: authored_neutral_global_pose_for_local_joint_drill. 2097.json#/name

Arms Hands

Hold one hand up in front of you with the palm facing you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2097.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2097.json#/name

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Hold one hand up in front of you with the palm facing you. 2097.json#/instructions/0
  2. Spread your fingers as far apart as feels comfortable. 2097.json#/instructions/1
  3. If you want more stretch, press between the fingers with your other hand. 2097.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep the wrist straight and the shoulder relaxed. 2097.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds. 2097.json#/instructions/4
  6. Relax the hand and switch sides. 2097.json#/instructions/5

2098 — adduction hand with elbow extended stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2098 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Tilts the hand sideways at the wrist with a straight elbow to stretch the forearm muscles along the outer edge of the arm.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand tall and extend one arm in front of you with the elbow straight.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand tall and extend one arm in front of you with the elbow straight. 2098.json#/instructions/0
  2. Turn the palm so the thumb points up. 2098.json#/instructions/1
  3. Grab the hand with your other hand and tilt it gently toward the little finger side. 2098.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep the elbow locked and the shoulder down. 2098.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds. 2098.json#/instructions/4
  6. Release and repeat on the other arm. 2098.json#/instructions/5

2099 — recumbent hip external rotator and hip extensor stretch (crossed leg)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2099 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A reclined figure four stretch for the buttocks and deep hip muscles.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with your knees bent and feet flat. Cross one ankle over the opposite knee, then pull the legs toward your chest until the buttock stretches.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2099.json#/instructions/0, 2099.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Rest the arms beside the torso with relaxed hands on the documented support surface; this is a balance/staging choice, not an added pressing action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with your knees bent and feet flat. 2099.json#/instructions/0
  2. Cross one ankle over the opposite knee, then pull the legs toward your chest until the buttock stretches. 2099.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your head and shoulders relaxed on the floor. 2099.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold for 20-30 seconds, feeling a gentle pull rather than pain. 2099.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release slowly and repeat, switching sides if the stretch is one sided. 2099.json#/instructions/4

2100 — seated quadriceps stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2100 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Stretches the front of the thigh from a seated position by folding one leg back while the other stays supported.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit sideways on a bench or sturdy seat with one leg hanging off the side.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2100.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit sideways on a bench or sturdy seat with one leg hanging off the side. 2100.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend the hanging knee and bring the heel back toward your glutes. 2100.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hold the foot or ankle with your hand and gently pull the heel closer. 2100.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your torso upright and your knees close together. 2100.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds. 2100.json#/instructions/4
  6. Release and switch legs. 2100.json#/instructions/5

2101 — front cross over shin stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2101 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Crossing one foot in front and pressing the toes down to stretch the front of the shin.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand tall and cross one leg in front of the other.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2101.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 2101.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2101.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand tall and cross one leg in front of the other. 2101.json#/instructions/0
  2. Point the toes of the back foot against the floor and gently bend the knee until the shin and ankle stretch. 2101.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hold a wall or chair for balance. 2101.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold for 20-30 seconds, feeling a gentle pull rather than pain. 2101.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release slowly and repeat, switching sides if the stretch is one sided. 2101.json#/instructions/4

2102 — sitting toe pull calf stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2102 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Pulls the toes back toward the shin while sitting, giving the calf and the back of the lower leg a gentle stretch.

Proposed staging

Sit on the floor with one leg straight out in front of you.

2104 — leaning heel back achilles stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2104 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Targets the lower calf and Achilles tendon area by leaning into a wall with the back heel pressed down and the knee bent.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand facing a wall and place your hands on it at chest height.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2104.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand facing a wall and place your hands on it at chest height.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2104.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2104.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand facing a wall and place your hands on it at chest height. 2104.json#/instructions/0
  2. Step one foot back and keep that heel on the floor. 2104.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bend both knees slightly and lean forward until you feel a pull low in the back calf. 2104.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep the back heel flat and the toes pointing forward. 2104.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds. 2104.json#/instructions/4
  6. Switch legs. 2104.json#/instructions/5
